# Agents · R · part 28

- **review-code-comments**: Check if changes comply with guidance in code comments ([prodelt/elt-harness](https://github.com/prodelt/elt-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/review-code-comments.md) · ★1)
- **review-code-comments**: Reviews the code comments a pull request adds or leaves behind. Checks every claim a comment makes against the source that settles it, and asks for the removal of comments that restate the code rather than for shorter ones. Spawned by a PR-review orchestrator that supplies the PR specifics; returns findings as structured comments and never writes to GitHub or Slack. ([martinjlowm/nixfiles](https://github.com/martinjlowm/nixfiles/blob/HEAD/config/claude/agents/review-code-comments.md))
- **review-code-correctness**: A code review agent that checks analysis scripts for logical errors, incorrect function usage, wrong variable references, off-by-one errors, silent coercion bugs, and data manipulation mistakes that could produce wrong results. Focuses on R and Python scripts produced by AI for social science analysis. ([joshzyj/open-scholar-skill](https://github.com/joshzyj/open-scholar-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-code-correctness.md) · ★193)
- **review-code-critical**: Performs adversarial code reviews focused on regressions, security, and missing test coverage. ([flaming-codes/sveltekit-runelayer](https://github.com/flaming-codes/sveltekit-runelayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-code-critical.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-code-data-handling**: A code review agent that verifies variable construction, recoding, categorization, sample restrictions, and data transformations against codebooks, data dictionaries, and design documents. Catches miscoded categories, wrong value labels, reversed scales, incorrect aggregation, and mishandled missing value codes in social science datasets. ([joshzyj/open-scholar-skill](https://github.com/joshzyj/open-scholar-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-code-data-handling.md) · ★193)
- **review-code-deep**: Generalist of ParleyInn's deep review for code and tests: weighs the findings of the low-effort specialists the review's orchestrator ran, confirms or drops each, adds what they missed and reports to the orchestrator. Never approves. ([jeduden/parleyinn](https://github.com/jeduden/parleyinn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-code-deep.md))
- **review-code-design**: Design-craft review — improvement hints against a stricter design ideal, not gatekeeping. Launched by /code-review:full; do not touch it outside of the code review workflow. - **review-communication**: Reads a message, email, PR or issue body, status update, incident update or public doc before it goes out, and returns PASS or REVISE. Checks audience, what leaked across the internal and external line, claims nothing backs, and the habits that make text read as generated. Read-only. Use on anything outward-facing, or on a draft the author is too close to judge. ([kingletas/organon](https://github.com/kingletas/organon/blob/HEAD/agents/review-communication.md))
- **review-companion**: Code review companion for any language. Supports a human reviewer with explained findings, recommendations and diagrams; assumes real use, assumes nothing else and never stops to ask; never approves. Use when a change touches anything another thing calls. ([kingletas/organon](https://github.com/kingletas/organon/blob/HEAD/agents/review-compat.md))

- **review-compatibility**: Reviews backward compatibility of a pytorch-bsf change: public API signatures and behavior, saved-model and file formats, CLI and MLflow entry points, and whether breaking changes are declared and documented.
